&#x20;         <div class="eva\_\_kicker">Questions</div>

&#x20;         <h2 class="eva\_\_h2">Frequently Asked Questions</h2>

&#x20;         <div class="eva\_\_divider"><span class="eva\_\_divider-dot"></span></div>

&#x20;       <div class="eva\_\_faq">



&#x20;         <div class="eva\_\_faq-item eva--open">

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-q">What is the difference between the two variants?<span class="eva\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

</div>

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-panel"><div class="eva\_\_faq-body">Variant 1 (<strong>Dye-Sub + PC Keycaps</strong>) includes the full Unit-01 keycap set — transparent PBT with metallic dye-sub printing plus PC polycarbonate top caps. Variant 2 (<strong>Metallic Keycaps + Full Wrist Rest</strong>) adds a matching full-size wrist rest for extended comfort during long typing sessions.</div></div>

&#x20;         </div>



&#x20;         <div class="eva\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-q">What does "transparent PBT" mean?<span class="eva\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

</div>

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-panel"><div class="eva\_\_faq-body">Transparent PBT means the keycap body is made from semi-transparent Polybutylene Terephthalate. Light passes through the material, creating a unique depth and glow effect — especially visible under desk or keyboard RGB lighting.</div></div>

&#x20;         </div>



&#x20;         <div class="eva\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-q">What switches are compatible?<span class="eva\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

</div>

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-panel"><div class="eva\_\_faq-body">Any standard MX-style mechanical switch — Cherry MX, Gateron, Kailh, Outemu and all MX-clone variants. Both standard cross-stem and box MX switches are supported. Not compatible with low-profile switches (e.g. Kailh Choc) or optical switches.</div></div>

&#x20;         </div>



&#x20;         <div class="eva\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-q">Which keyboard sizes and layouts is this set compatible with?<span class="eva\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

</div>

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-panel"><div class="eva\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;             This set works with virtually all keyboard sizes and layout standards:<br><br>

&#x20;             <strong>✓ Sizes:</strong> 60% · 65% · 68% · 75% · TKL · Full-size — all covered with the included key count and modifier set.<br>

&#x20;             <strong>✓ Layout standards:</strong> ANSI, ISO and Alice layouts are all supported.<br>

&#x20;             <strong>✓ Spacebars:</strong> Both <strong>6.25U and 7.0U</strong> are included, covering the two most common spacebar sizes.<br><br>

&#x20;             If you have a non-standard or boutique layout, feel free to message us with your board model and we will confirm compatibility before you order.

&#x20;           </div></div>

&#x20;         </div>



&#x20;         <div class="eva\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-q">Will the legends fade over time?<span class="eva\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

</div>

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-panel"><div class="eva\_\_faq-body">No. The metallic legends are applied via dye-sublimation, which fuses the ink directly into the PBT surface. The result is permanent and cannot peel, chip or fade with normal daily use.</div></div>

&#x20;         </div>



&#x20;         <div class="eva\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-q">Is the keyboard in the photos included?<span class="eva\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

</div>

&#x20;           <div class="eva\_\_faq-panel"><div class="eva\_\_faq-body">No — this listing is for the keycap set only (or the keycap set + wrist rest in Variant 2). The keyboard, case, cables and all other props shown are for display purposes only.</div></div>

&#x20;         </div>

<script>

(function(){

&#x20; /\* FAQ accordion \*/

&#x20; var items = document.querySelectorAll('.eva\_\_faq-item');

&#x20; items.forEach(function(item){

&#x20;   var panel = item.querySelector('.eva\_\_faq-panel');

&#x20;   if(!panel) return;

&#x20;   if(item.classList.contains('eva--open')){ panel.style.maxHeight=panel.scrollHeight+'px'; panel.style.opacity='1'; }

&#x20;   else { panel.style.maxHeight='0px'; panel.style.opacity='0'; }

&#x20;   item.querySelector('.eva\_\_faq-q').addEventListener('click',function(){

&#x20;     var isOpen=item.classList.contains('eva--open');

&#x20;     items.forEach(function(el){ el.classList.remove('eva--open'); var p=el.querySelector('.eva\_\_faq-panel'); if(p){p.style.maxHeight='0px';p.style.opacity='0';} });

&#x20;     if(!isOpen){ item.classList.add('eva--open'); panel.style.maxHeight=panel.scrollHeight+'px'; panel.style.opacity='1'; }

&#x20;   });

&#x20; });



&#x20; /\* Add to Cart — smooth scroll + soft glow on the store's own button \*/

&#x20; var btn = document.getElementById('evaCartBtn');

&#x20; if(!btn) return;

&#x20; btn.addEventListener('click', function(e){

&#x20;   e.preventDefault();

&#x20;   var selectors = \[

&#x20;     'button\[name="add"]',

&#x20;     '\[name="add"]',

&#x20;     '.product-form\_\_submit',

&#x20;     '#AddToCart',

&#x20;     '.btn--add-to-cart',

&#x20;     'form\[action\*="/cart/add"] \[type="submit"]'

&#x20;   ];

&#x20;   var target = null;

&#x20;   for(var i=0; i<selectors.length; i++){

&#x20;     var el = document.querySelector(selectors\[i]);

&#x20;     if(el \&\& window.getComputedStyle(el).display !== 'none'){ target = el; break; }

&#x20;   }

&#x20;   if(!target) return;



&#x20;   target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });



&#x20;   setTimeout(function(){

&#x20;     var prev = {

&#x20;       transition: target.style.transition,

&#x20;       boxShadow:  target.style.boxShadow,

&#x20;       transform:  target.style.transform,

&#x20;       outline:    target.style.outline

&#x20;     };

&#x20;     /\* Ease in with a gentle glow — no jarring scale \*/

&#x20;     target.style.transition = 'box-shadow 1.0s cubic-bezier(0.25,0.1,0.25,1), transform 0.7s cubic-bezier(0.25,0.1,0.25,1), outline 0.8s ease';

&#x20;     target.style.boxShadow  = '0 0 0 3px rgba(107,47,160,0.22), 0 0 26px rgba(107,47,160,0.38), 0 6px 18px rgba(107,47,160,0.18)';

&#x20;     target.style.transform  = 'scale(1.015)';

&#x20;     target.style.outline    = '2px solid rgba(107,47,160,0.40)';

&#x20;     /\* Settle to a whisper glow \*/

&#x20;     setTimeout(function(){

&#x20;       target.style.boxShadow = '0 0 0 2px rgba(107,47,160,0.14), 0 0 16px rgba(107,47,160,0.24)';

&#x20;       target.style.transform  = 'scale(1)';

&#x20;       target.style.outline    = '1px solid rgba(107,47,160,0.25)';

&#x20;     }, 1100);

&#x20;     /\* Fade out completely \*/

&#x20;     setTimeout(function(){

&#x20;       target.style.transition = 'box-shadow 1.2s ease, outline 1.0s ease';

&#x20;       target.style.boxShadow  = prev.boxShadow;

&#x20;       target.style.outline    = prev.outline;

&#x20;     }, 4000);

&#x20;     /\* Restore all original styles \*/

&#x20;     setTimeout(function(){

&#x20;       target.style.transition = prev.transition;

&#x20;       target.style.transform  = prev.transform;

&#x20;     }, 5200);

&#x20;   }, 900);

&#x20; });

})();

</script>

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════ THE STORY ═══════════════════════════════ -->

<section class="qm\_\_section">

&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_head">

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_kicker">The Inspiration</div>

&#x20;   <h2 class="qm\_\_h2">From Ancient Ink to Modern Keys</h2>

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_divider"><span class="qm\_\_divider-dot"></span></div>

&#x20; </div>



&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_prose">

&#x20;   <p>

&#x20;     Ancient China gave the world lacquerware, silk dyes and imperial pigments of extraordinary depth.

&#x20;     Artisans ground lapis and madder root into colour that has endured for thousands of years on

&#x20;     bronze vessels, jade screens and palace walls. The <strong>Ancient Chinese Theme Keycap Set</strong>

&#x20;     carries that legacy forward — every key a brushstroke, the full board a canvas that shifts from

&#x20;     deep imperial purple at the escape key through twilight violet across the alphas and into absolute

&#x20;     ink black at the lower rows.

&#x20;   </p>

&#x20;   <p>

&#x20;     This is not a cosmetic gradient. It is a deliberate chromatic arc that mirrors the moment a master

&#x20;     calligrapher's brush lifts from an ink stone, trailing pigment from its richest, most saturated

&#x20;     bloom into darkness — focused, controlled, inevitable. Placed on your desk, the set reads as a

&#x20;     single unified composition rather than a collection of individual keys.

&#x20;   </p>

&#x20;   <p>

&#x20;     The design language draws on oracle-bone script, lacquerware geometry and the negative-space

&#x20;     philosophy of Chinese brush painting: let the darkness speak. The result is a board that commands

&#x20;     attention without ornamentation — quiet confidence, deep craft.

&#x20;   </p>

&#x20; </div>

</section>



<!-- ═══════════════════════════════ COLOUR STORY ═══════════════════════════════ -->

<section class="qm\_\_section">

&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_head">

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_kicker">Colour Palette</div>

&#x20;   <h2 class="qm\_\_h2">The Gradient, Explained</h2>

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_divider"><span class="qm\_\_divider-dot"></span></div>

&#x20; </div>



&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_colour-story">

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_colour-bar">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_colour-swatch">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_colour-label">Purple · Violet · Ink · Black</div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_colour-steps">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_colour-step">

&#x20;         <div class="qm\_\_colour-dot" style="background:#4A0E6B;"></div>

&#x20;         <span style="color:var(--qm-text);font-weight:800;">Imperial</span>

&#x20;         <span>Purple</span>

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_colour-step">

&#x20;         <div class="qm\_\_colour-dot" style="background:#2D0A3A;"></div>

&#x20;         <span style="color:var(--qm-text);font-weight:800;">Deep</span>

&#x20;         <span>Violet</span>

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_colour-step">

&#x20;         <div class="qm\_\_colour-dot" style="background:#140820;"></div>

&#x20;         <span style="color:var(--qm-text);font-weight:800;">Midnight</span>

&#x20;         <span>Ink</span>

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_colour-step">

&#x20;         <div class="qm\_\_colour-dot" style="background:#0A0A0F;border:1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12);"></div>

&#x20;         <span style="color:var(--qm-text);font-weight:800;">Absolute</span>

&#x20;         <span>Black</span>

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_colour-text">

&#x20;     <h3>Inspired by Imperial Lacquerware</h3>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       The palette opens at <strong>Imperial Purple (#4A0E6B)</strong> — the colour reserved for dynasty

&#x20;       and nobility in classical Chinese culture — present on the function row and escape key where your

&#x20;       eye naturally lands first.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       Across the number row and upper alphas the colour deepens to <strong>Deep Violet (#2D0A3A)</strong>,

&#x20;       evoking the moment lacquer begins to cure and darken, becoming richer and more complex.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       The lower alphas and modifiers transition through <strong>Midnight Ink (#140820)</strong> — the

&#x20;       exact shade of freshly ground ink on a Han-dynasty ink stone — before arriving at

&#x20;       <strong>Absolute Black (#0A0A0F)</strong> on the bottom row: total darkness, total stillness.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       <strong>No green. No teal. No cool-blue undertone.</strong> The entire arc is warm-purple to

&#x20;       warm-black — an intentional choice that reads as classical rather than synthetic.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;   </div>

&#x20; </div>

</section>

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════ FEATURES ═══════════════════════════════ -->

<section class="qm\_\_section">

&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_head">

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_kicker">What Sets It Apart</div>

&#x20;   <h2 class="qm\_\_h2">Six Reasons to Choose This Set</h2>

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_divider"><span class="qm\_\_divider-dot"></span></div>

&#x20; </div>



&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_features">



&#x20;   <article class="qm\_\_feature">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon">🔥</div>

&#x20;     <h3>Full Five-Side Heat Sublimation</h3>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       Thermal dye-sub covers all five faces of every keycap — top, front, left, right and back.

&#x20;       The gradient wraps the entire key body, reads cleanly from every angle, and cannot peel

&#x20;       or chip because the colour is fused into the PBT at a molecular level.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;   </article>



&#x20;   <article class="qm\_\_feature">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon">🧱</div>

&#x20;     <h3>Premium PBT Plastic</h3>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       PBT (Polybutylene Terephthalate) is harder, denser and more heat-stable than ABS.

&#x20;       It resists the shine, yellowing and finger-oil absorption that plagues cheaper caps —

&#x20;       keeping your set looking as crisp on day 1,000 as it did on day one.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;   </article>



&#x20;   <article class="qm\_\_feature">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon">📐</div>

&#x20;     <h3>Cherry Profile Sculpt</h3>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       Cherry profile is a low-to-mid-height classic: a slightly angled top surface that guides

&#x20;       your fingertips to the centre of each key, a satisfying travel depth, and a grounded

&#x20;       thock that stays composed at any typing pace.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;   </article>



&#x20;   <article class="qm\_\_feature">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon">🗝️</div>

&#x20;     <h3>172 Keys — One Set, Every Build</h3>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       A 172-key count is rare at this price point. Extra modifiers, alternate legends, ISO keys,

&#x20;       Alice-specific keys and a 7U spacebar are all included so you can fit virtually any

&#x20;       standard layout without hunting for add-on kits.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;   </article>



&#x20;   <article class="qm\_\_feature">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon">🔩</div>

&#x20;     <h3>Universal MX Compatibility</h3>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       Standard MX cross-stem mount fits Cherry MX, Gateron, Kailh, Outemu, Durock and

&#x20;       every other MX-clone switch. Box MX switches are also supported. Works across

&#x20;       hot-swap and soldered PCBs alike.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;   </article>



&#x20;   <article class="qm\_\_feature">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon">🛡️</div>

&#x20;     <h3>Oil-Resistant Surface Coating</h3>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       An anti-oil treatment on the keycap surface repels skin oils and daily grime,

&#x20;       maintaining the matte finish and deep colour fidelity for years of heavy use — no

&#x20;       polishing, no greasy high-traffic keys.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;   </article>



&#x20; </div>

</section>



<!-- ═══════════════════════════════ SPOTLIGHT — MATERIAL ═══════════════════════════════ -->

<section class="qm\_\_section">

&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_spotlight">

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_kicker">Material Science</div>

&#x20;   <h2>PBT That Goes the Distance</h2>

&#x20;   <p>

&#x20;     Not all PBT is equal. Thin-walled PBT flexes and produces a hollow, tinny sound.

&#x20;     This set uses <strong>thick-wall PBT stock</strong> — the walls are substantial enough

&#x20;     to produce a dense, satisfying thock, to hold the heat-sublimation dye without

&#x20;     colour bleed, and to resist the micro-abrasion of ten-thousand daily keystrokes

&#x20;     without thinning. Combined with the oil-resistant coating, the surface stays

&#x20;     perfectly matte: no polish, no gloss, no compromise.

&#x20;   </p>



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_spec-grid">

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════ CHERRY PROFILE DETAIL ═══════════════════════════════ -->

<section class="qm\_\_section">

&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_head">

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_kicker">Profile Deep Dive</div>

&#x20;   <h2 class="qm\_\_h2">Why Cherry Profile Works</h2>

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_divider"><span class="qm\_\_divider-dot"></span></div>

&#x20; </div>



&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_two-col">

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_dark-prose">

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       <strong>Cherry profile</strong> is one of the most refined sculpted profiles in mechanical

&#x20;       keyboard history. Developed over decades of commercial keyboard design, it sits lower than

&#x20;       OEM profile — reducing the risk of strain during extended sessions — while maintaining enough

&#x20;       height for a full, satisfying key-travel sensation.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       Each row has a specific sculpt angle: the function row is relatively flat, the number row

&#x20;       angles slightly backward, and the rows below it progressively tilt forward, guiding the hand

&#x20;       into a natural resting arch. The result is a cohesive typing experience where each finger

&#x20;       meets its key at the ideal contact angle without compensation.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;     <p>

&#x20;       <strong>Sound character:</strong> Cherry profile's shorter walls compared to SA or MT3 let

&#x20;       switch sound travel directly upward rather than resonating inside a tall key chamber. Combined

&#x20;       with thick-wall PBT, this produces a clean, focused thock — present but never boomy.

&#x20;     </p>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20;   <div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_features" style="flex-direction:column; gap:12px; margin-top:0;">

&#x20;       <article class="qm\_\_feature" style="max-width:100%; flex:none; text-align:left; padding:18px 20px;">

&#x20;         <div style="display:flex; gap:14px; align-items:flex-start;">

&#x20;           <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon" style="flex:none; margin:0;">🎯</div>

&#x20;           <div>

&#x20;             <h3 style="text-align:left;">Accurate Finger Placement</h3>

&#x20;             <p>Sculpted rows guide each finger to the centre of its key with minimal lateral drift.</p>

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </div>

&#x20;       </article>

&#x20;       <article class="qm\_\_feature" style="max-width:100%; flex:none; text-align:left; padding:18px 20px;">

&#x20;         <div style="display:flex; gap:14px; align-items:flex-start;">

&#x20;           <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon" style="flex:none; margin:0;">📏</div>

&#x20;           <div>

&#x20;             <h3 style="text-align:left;">Low-Profile Comfort</h3>

&#x20;             <p>Lower than OEM, reducing wrist extension and shoulder strain during marathon sessions.</p>

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </div>

&#x20;       </article>

&#x20;       <article class="qm\_\_feature" style="max-width:100%; flex:none; text-align:left; padding:18px 20px;">

&#x20;         <div style="display:flex; gap:14px; align-items:flex-start;">

&#x20;           <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon" style="flex:none; margin:0;">🔊</div>

&#x20;           <div>

&#x20;             <h3 style="text-align:left;">Clean Acoustic Signature</h3>

&#x20;             <p>Focused, composed thock — never hollow or plastic-sounding.</p>

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </div>

&#x20;       </article>

&#x20;       <article class="qm\_\_feature" style="max-width:100%; flex:none; text-align:left; padding:18px 20px;">

&#x20;         <div style="display:flex; gap:14px; align-items:flex-start;">

&#x20;           <div class="qm\_\_feature-icon" style="flex:none; margin:0;">🤝</div>

&#x20;           <div>

&#x20;             <h3 style="text-align:left;">Widely Familiar</h3>

&#x20;             <p>Standard in commercial keyboards — no adaptation period for most typists.</p>

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </div>

&#x20;       </article>

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════ FAQ ═══════════════════════════════ -->

<section class="qm\_\_section">

&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_head">

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_kicker">Questions \&amp; Answers</div>

&#x20;   <h2 class="qm\_\_h2">Frequently Asked Questions</h2>

&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_divider"><span class="qm\_\_divider-dot"></span></div>

&#x20; </div>



&#x20; <div class="qm\_\_faq">



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_faq-item qm--open">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-q">

&#x20;       What exactly is "five-side heat sublimation" and why does it matter?

&#x20;       <span class="qm\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-panel">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;         Most keycap sets print only the top surface — the legend you see when looking down. Five-side

&#x20;         heat sublimation prints all five exposed faces of each keycap: top, front, back, left and right. The

&#x20;         process works by pressing a specially prepared transfer paper against the keycap surface at high

&#x20;         temperature. The dye sublimates — turns directly from solid to gas — and bonds into the PBT at a

&#x20;         molecular level, becoming part of the material rather than a layer on top of it.<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>Why it matters:</strong> The colour cannot peel, chip, crack or fade with normal use.

&#x20;         The gradient reads seamlessly from every angle — when you look at your keys from the side,

&#x20;         you see the same deep purple-to-black arc as from above. It is the most durable and visually

&#x20;         consistent printing method available for keycaps.

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-q">

&#x20;       Which keyboard layouts and sizes does this set support?

&#x20;       <span class="qm\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-panel">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;         The 172-key count provides coverage for all major standard layouts:<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>Layout standards:</strong> ANSI · ISO · Alice — all three are covered with

&#x20;         dedicated keys included in the package.<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>Form factor sizes:</strong> 60% · 65% · 68% · 75% · TKL (80%) · Full-size (100%) · Alice.<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>Spacebar:</strong> Both a standard 6.25U and a 7U spacebar are included,

&#x20;         covering virtually all standard and wider-format boards.<br><br>

&#x20;         If you have a boutique or highly non-standard layout (e.g. Ortholinear, Numpad-only, split ergonomic),

&#x20;         please contact us with your specific key list before ordering and we will confirm compatibility.

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-q">

&#x20;       What switch types are compatible?

&#x20;       <span class="qm\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-panel">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;         Any switch with a standard MX cross-stem mount is compatible:<br><br>

&#x20;         ✓ Cherry MX (all variants — Red, Brown, Blue, Black, Silent, Speed, etc.)<br>

&#x20;         ✓ Gateron (standard and milky housing, all weights)<br>

&#x20;         ✓ Kailh (standard box and non-box MX stems)<br>

&#x20;         ✓ Outemu, Akko, Durock, Boba U4/U4T and all MX clones<br>

&#x20;         ✓ Box MX switches (Kailh Box, etc.)<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>Not compatible:</strong> Low-profile switches (Kailh Choc, Gateron KS-33 Low Profile),

&#x20;         optical switches without MX stems, Alps SKCL/SKCM, Topre, membrane domes, or laptop butterfly/scissor mechanisms.

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-q">

&#x20;       Will the colours fade, shine or yellow over time?

&#x20;       <span class="qm\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-panel">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;         No — and there are two independent reasons why.<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>Fade:</strong> Heat sublimation bonds the dye into the PBT material itself, not onto the

&#x20;         surface. There is no coating or ink layer to flake. The colour is as permanent as the plastic.

&#x20;         Even after years of daily use under direct light, the gradient will remain consistent.<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>Shine \&amp; yellowing:</strong> This is a PBT-specific advantage. ABS plastic, used in many

&#x20;         cheaper keycap sets, contains additives that react with UV light and oxidise over time, turning yellow

&#x20;         and developing a slick, shiny surface. PBT does not contain these additives. It is also significantly

&#x20;         harder, so it resists the polishing effect of fingertip oils and daily friction. The oil-resistant

&#x20;         surface coating adds a further barrier. The matte texture you feel on day one is the same texture

&#x20;         you will feel in two years.

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-q">

&#x20;       What is Cherry profile and how does it compare to OEM or SA?

&#x20;       <span class="qm\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-panel">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;         Keycap profiles define the height and angle of each key row, which affects ergonomics, sound and feel.<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>Cherry profile</strong> sits lower than OEM — the keycaps are shorter, which reduces the

&#x20;         distance your wrists must angle upward. Each row is sculpted at a slightly different angle to match

&#x20;         the natural curve of the hand. It is widely considered the "most neutral" sculpted profile: familiar

&#x20;         enough for most typists to adapt to immediately, comfortable for long sessions, and acoustically

&#x20;         clean due to shorter key walls.<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>vs OEM:</strong> OEM is taller and less sculpted. Many mass-market boards ship with OEM.

&#x20;         Cherry feels slightly lower and more precise by comparison.<br><br>

&#x20;         <strong>vs SA:</strong> SA is very tall and spherical-top. It is visually dramatic but requires more

&#x20;         adaptation for fast typists. Cherry is faster to learn and more suitable for productivity use.

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-q">

&#x20;       Is the 7U spacebar included, and which boards need it?

&#x20;       <span class="qm\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-panel">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;         Yes — a <strong>7U spacebar is included</strong> in this 172-key set at no extra cost.<br><br>

&#x20;         Most standard keyboards use a 6.25U spacebar. Some older designs — including certain full-size

&#x20;         boards and some budget keyboards — use a wider 7U spacebar. If you are unsure which size your

&#x20;         board uses, check your keyboard's product listing or measure your existing spacebar: a 7U spacebar

&#x20;         is noticeably wider than the standard 6.25U.<br><br>

&#x20;         Alice-layout boards typically use a split spacebar (two separate keys), and Alice-specific

&#x20;         spacebar keys are also included in this set.

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-q">

&#x20;       Is the keyboard shown in the product photos included?

&#x20;       <span class="qm\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-panel">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;         No. This listing is for the <strong>keycap set only</strong> — 172 keycaps in a protective foam tray.<br><br>

&#x20;         The keyboard cases, boards, switches, cables, desk mats, wrist rests and all other accessories or

&#x20;         props visible in the product photography are included for visual reference only to show how the

&#x20;         set looks when installed. They are not shipped with your order.

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20;   <div class="qm\_\_faq-item">

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-q">

&#x20;       How is the gradient applied across the rows?

&#x20;       <span class="qm\_\_faq-icon">+</span>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="qm\_\_faq-panel">

&#x20;       <div class="qm\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;         The gradient is applied at the individual key level using the heat-sublimation process — each

&#x20;         key receives a precise section of the overall colour arc based on its row position.<br><br>

&#x20;         The palette flows from top to bottom of the board:<br><br>

&#x20;         🟣 <strong>Function row / Escape:</strong> Imperial Purple (#4A0E6B) — the richest, most saturated tone.<br>

&#x20;         🟣 <strong>Number row / Upper alphas:</strong> Deep Violet (#2D0A3A) — cooler and darker.<br>

&#x20;         ⬛ <strong>Home row / Mid alphas:</strong> Midnight Ink (#140820) — deep, near-black purple.<br>

&#x20;         ⬛ <strong>Lower rows / Modifiers:</strong> Absolute Black (#0A0A0F) — pure darkness.<br><br>

&#x20;         Because five-side sublimation wraps all faces, the gradient reads consistently whether you are

&#x20;         looking down at the keys, viewing the board from the side, or examining individual keycaps up close.

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>



&#x20; </div>

</section>

<script>

(function(){

&#x20; document.querySelectorAll('.qm\_\_faq-item').forEach(function(item){

&#x20;   var q = item.querySelector('.qm\_\_faq-q');

&#x20;   var panel = item.querySelector('.qm\_\_faq-panel');

&#x20;   // open default item

&#x20;   if(item.classList.contains('qm--open') \&\& panel){

&#x20;     panel.style.maxHeight = panel.scrollHeight + 'px';

&#x20;     panel.style.opacity = '1';

&#x20;   }

&#x20;   if(q \&\& panel){

&#x20;     q.addEventListener('click', function(){

&#x20;       var isOpen = item.classList.contains('qm--open');

&#x20;       // close all

&#x20;       document.querySelectorAll('.qm\_\_faq-item').forEach(function(i){

&#x20;         i.classList.remove('qm--open');

&#x20;         var p = i.querySelector('.qm\_\_faq-panel');

&#x20;         if(p){ p.style.maxHeight = '0'; p.style.opacity = '0'; }

&#x20;       });

&#x20;       if(!isOpen){

&#x20;         item.classList.add('qm--open');

&#x20;         panel.style.maxHeight = panel.scrollHeight + 'px';

&#x20;         panel.style.opacity = '1';

&#x20;       }

&#x20;     });

&#x20;   }

&#x20; });

})();

</script>

<!-- THE POEM -->

<section class="mu2-section on-brown">

&#x20; <div class="mu2-wrap">

&#x20;   <div class="mu2-poem">

&#x20;     <span class="mu2-poem-tag">The Inspiration</span>

&#x20;     <h2>Autumn Twilight in the Mountain Abode</h2>

&#x20;     <div class="cn">山居秋暝 · Wang Wei</div>

&#x20;     <p>The keycap set is drawn from Wang Wei's Tang-dynasty poem — fresh rain over empty mountains, moonlight through pines, a clear stream running over stone. Chinese landscape brushwork carries that stillness across the alphas, the spacebar, and the larger function row this 75% layout adds.</p>

&#x20;   </div>

&#x20; </div>

</section>

<!-- CARE \& MAINTENANCE -->

<section class="mu2-section">

&#x20; <div class="mu2-wrap">

&#x20;   <div class="mu2-head">

&#x20;     <span class="mu2-tag">Keep It Beautiful</span>

&#x20;     <h2>Caring for the Walnut Case</h2>

&#x20;   </div>

&#x20;   <div class="mu2-card-grid">

&#x20;     <div class="mu2-card">

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-card-num">01</div>

&#x20;       <h4>Wax Occasionally</h4>

&#x20;       <p>Apply the included wood wax lightly with the included cloth every few months to keep the walnut conditioned and rich in tone.</p>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="mu2-card">

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-card-num">02</div>

&#x20;       <h4>Avoid Direct Sunlight</h4>

&#x20;       <p>Prolonged sun exposure can dry out or discolor the wood over time — keep the board out of direct light when possible.</p>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="mu2-card">

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-card-num">03</div>

&#x20;       <h4>Keep It Dry</h4>

&#x20;       <p>Wipe up spills immediately and avoid placing drinks directly beside the case to protect the wood finish.</p>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;     <div class="mu2-card">

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-card-num">04</div>

&#x20;       <h4>Recalibrate After Swaps</h4>

&#x20;       <p>After hot-swapping switches, give the board a quick check in Akko Cloud Driver to confirm each key registers cleanly.</p>

&#x20;     </div>

&#x20;   </div>

&#x20; </div>

</section>

<!-- FAQ -->

<section class="mu2-section on-brown">

&#x20; <div class="mu2-wrap">

&#x20;   <div class="mu2-head">

&#x20;     <span class="mu2-tag">Answers</span>

&#x20;     <h2>Frequently Asked Questions</h2>

&#x20;   </div>

&#x20;   <div class="mu2-faq">

&#x20;     <details open="">

&#x20;       <summary>How is MU02 different from MU01 Mountain Seclusion?</summary>

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-faq-body">MU02 uses a 75% layout with an added function row instead of MU01's 65% layout, and pairs an aluminum top plate with the walnut wood bottom case rather than an all-wood shell. Both share the same Mountain Seclusion keycap theme and Akko Rosewood switch.</div>

&#x20;     </details>

&#x20;     <details>

&#x20;       <summary>Is this a full solid wood case?</summary>

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-faq-body">The bottom case is CNC-machined from solid walnut wood, paired with an aluminum top plate. Natural grain and slight tone variation between units are expected with real wood.</div>

&#x20;     </details>

&#x20;     <details>

&#x20;       <summary>Can I swap the Akko Rosewood switches for something else?</summary>

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-faq-body">Yes. The PCB is hot-swap compatible, so you can install other compatible mechanical switches without soldering.</div>

&#x20;     </details>

&#x20;     <details>

&#x20;       <summary>Does the keyboard work on Mac?</summary>

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-faq-body">Yes, MU02 supports both Windows and macOS with an easy system switch.</div>

&#x20;     </details>

&#x20;     <details>

&#x20;       <summary>How long does the battery last?</summary>

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-faq-body">The 4,000mAh battery is built for extended wireless use across both Bluetooth and 2.4GHz modes; actual runtime depends on backlight brightness and usage.</div>

&#x20;     </details>

&#x20;     <details>

&#x20;       <summary>Will I always receive the wood wax and cloth?</summary>

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-faq-body">Most orders include a wood wax block and polishing cloth for case maintenance, but these may be removed from packages shipping to regions with restrictions on wax products.</div>

&#x20;     </details>

&#x20;     <details>

&#x20;       <summary>Is this keyboard part of a licensed collaboration?</summary>

&#x20;       <div class="mu2-faq-body">No — Mountain Seclusion is an original Akko design inspired by classical Chinese poetry and landscape painting, not a licensed third-party collaboration.</div>

&#x20;     </details>

&#x20;   </div>

&#x20; </div>

</section>

&#x20;   for (var i = 0; i < ATC.length; i++) { var el = document.querySelector(ATC\[i]); if (el) return el; }

&#x20;   return null;

&#x20; }

&#x20; function jumpToATC() {

&#x20;   var btn = findATC();

&#x20;   if (btn) {

&#x20;   }

&#x20; }

<div class="smrz\_\_prose">

<p>This is not a printed novelty cap. The <strong>Super Mario Bros. Layered Resin Artisan Keycap</strong> is hand-built using a layered drip-resin technique — tiny pours of tinted resin stacked and cured one at a time until a character emerges with real depth, shading and dimension instead of a flat sticker-like image.</p>

<p>Choose your hero: <strong>Mario</strong>, <strong>Luigi</strong>, <strong>Yoshi</strong> or <strong>Princess Peach</strong> — each sold as an individual artisan piece, sized to sit perfectly on an <strong>ESC key</strong> or any other key on your board, mounted on a standard cross-stem base.</p>

<section class="smrz\_\_section">

<div class="smrz\_\_spotlight">

<div class="smrz\_\_kicker">The Signature Technique</div>

<h2>Built in Layers. Not Printed on Top.</h2>

<p>Most novelty keycaps print a flat image and call it a day. This one is different — each character is formed by pouring small amounts of tinted resin in stages, letting each layer set before the next colour goes on, the same patient technique used by diorama artists. The result is a cap with real depth: shading that sits inside the plastic, and a glassy finish that catches the light from every angle.</p>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-grid">

<div class="smrz\_\_spec">

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-k">Technique</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-v">Layered Drip Resin</div>

</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec">

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-k">Material</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-v">Resin + PBT Base</div>

</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec">

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-k">Model</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-v">Super Mario Bros.</div>

</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec">

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-k">Mount</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-v">Cross Stem (MX-Style)</div>

</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec">

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-k">Fit</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_spec-v">ESC Key \&amp; Standard Keys</div>

</div>

<section class="smrz\_\_section">

<div class="smrz\_\_head">

<div class="smrz\_\_kicker">Easy Setup</div>

<h2 class="smrz\_\_h2">Install in Three Steps</h2>

<p class="smrz\_\_lead">No tools beyond a standard keycap puller. Done in seconds.</p>

<div class="smrz\_\_divider">

<span class="smrz\_\_diamond"></span><br>

</div>

</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_steps">

<article class="smrz\_\_step">

<div class="smrz\_\_step-num">01</div>

<h4>Pull the Old Cap</h4>

<p>Slide a wire keycap puller over your current ESC cap (or whichever key you've chosen) and lift it straight up.</p>

</article>

<article class="smrz\_\_step">

<div class="smrz\_\_step-num">02</div>

<h4>Line It Up</h4>

<p>Hold the new resin keycap so the character faces forward, and centre it over the exposed switch stem.</p>

</article>

<article class="smrz\_\_step">

<div class="smrz\_\_step-num">03</div>

<h4>Press Into Place</h4>

<p>Press down gently and evenly until the cap clicks securely onto the cross stem. Power-up complete.</p>

</article>

</div>

</section>

<section class="smrz\_\_section">

<div class="smrz\_\_head">

<div class="smrz\_\_kicker">Keep It Looking Sharp</div>

<h2 class="smrz\_\_h2">Care \&amp; Keeping</h2>

<div class="smrz\_\_divider">

<span class="smrz\_\_diamond"></span><br>

</div>

</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_care">

<article class="smrz\_\_care-card">

<div class="smrz\_\_care-num">01</div>

<h4>Gentle Dusting</h4>

<p>Wipe with a soft, dry or barely damp cloth to keep the glossy resin top free of dust and fingerprints.</p>

</article>

<article class="smrz\_\_care-card">

<div class="smrz\_\_care-num">02</div>

<h4>Avoid Harsh Chemicals</h4>

<p>Skip solvents, alcohol wipes and abrasive cleaners — they can dull or cloud the cured resin surface.</p>

</article>

<article class="smrz\_\_care-card">

<div class="smrz\_\_care-num">03</div>

<h4>Use a Puller</h4>

<p>Always remove the cap with a wire keycap puller rather than prying with your fingernail.</p>

</article>

<article class="smrz\_\_care-card">

<div class="smrz\_\_care-num">04</div>

<h4>Keep Out of Direct Sun</h4>

<p>Prolonged direct sunlight can yellow resin over time — keep your board out of harsh UV exposure.</p>

</article>

</div>

</section>

<section class="smrz\_\_section">

<div class="smrz\_\_head">

<div class="smrz\_\_kicker">Questions?</div>

<h2 class="smrz\_\_h2">Frequently Asked Questions</h2>

<div class="smrz\_\_divider">

<span class="smrz\_\_diamond"></span><br>

</div>

</div>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq">

<details open="">

<summary>What does "layered resin" actually mean?</summary>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq-body">Instead of printing a flat image onto the cap, the character is built by pouring small amounts of tinted resin in stages, letting each layer cure before adding the next. This gives the artwork real physical depth and a glassy finish rather than a flat printed surface.</div>

</details>

<details>

<summary>Will it fit my switches?</summary>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq-body">It uses a standard cross stem and fits Cherry MX, Gateron, Kailh and most MX-compatible switches. If you're unsure about your board, message us with your keyboard model and we'll confirm before you order.</div>

</details>

<details>

<summary>Can I buy all four characters together?</summary>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq-body">Each character — Mario, Luigi, Yoshi and Princess Peach — is listed and sold individually. To collect the full crew, simply add each character to your cart separately.</div>

</details>

<details>

<summary>Is it compatible with RGB / backlit keyboards?</summary>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq-body">The keycap is made from opaque, tinted resin and does not let RGB light shine through — backlit compatible: No. It still fits physically on any MX board, but the RGB effect is blocked underneath the cap.</div>

</details>

<details>

<summary>Which key should I put it on?</summary>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq-body">It's sized to sit beautifully on a standard ESC key, the most popular spot for artisan keycaps, but it fits any standard-size 1×1 key on your board.</div>

</details>

<details>

<summary>Is this an official Nintendo / Mario product?</summary>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq-body">This is a third-party artisan keycap inspired by the Super Mario Bros. universe. It is not an officially licensed Nintendo product and is sold as a fan-made collectible accessory.</div>

</details>

<details>

<summary>How do I install or remove it?</summary>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq-body">Press the cap down over the cross stem of your chosen key until it clicks into place. To remove it, use a wire keycap puller rather than prying with your fingers.</div>

</details>

<details>

<summary>Will the resin yellow or fade over time?</summary>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq-body">High-quality resin can discolour with prolonged, constant direct UV exposure. Keep your board out of harsh, continuous sunlight to preserve the clarity and colour of the resin.</div>

</details>

<details>

<summary>Is the keyboard included?</summary>

<div class="smrz\_\_faq-body">No — this listing is for the keycap only. The keyboard, switches and any props shown in the photos are for display purposes and are not included in your purchase.</div>

</details>

</div>

</section>

&#x20;     <!-- =================== CRAFTSMANSHIP =================== -->

&#x20;     <section class="hpkc\_\_section">

&#x20;       <div class="hpkc\_\_spotlight">

&#x20;         <div class="hpkc\_\_kicker">Crafted to Endure</div>

&#x20;         <h2>Forged in PBT, Sealed by Dye-Sublimation</h2>

&#x20;         <p>

&#x20;           Lesser keycaps rely on coatings and print-on-top legends that fade,

&#x20;           grease over and shine within months. This set is built differently.

&#x20;           Dense PBT plastic delivers a deep, deadened sound and a textured feel,

&#x20;           while dye-sublimated legends are infused into the surface itself —

&#x20;           never to peel, smudge or vanish.

&#x20;         </p>

&#x20;     <!-- =================== SOUND \& FEEL =================== -->

&#x20;     <section class="hpkc\_\_section">

&#x20;       <div class="hpkc\_\_head">

&#x20;         <div class="hpkc\_\_kicker">The Experience</div>

&#x20;         <h2 class="hpkc\_\_h2">Sound \&amp; Feel</h2>

&#x20;         <p class="hpkc\_\_lead">More than a look — these keycaps change how your

&#x20;           board sounds and feels under your fingers.</p>

&#x20;         <div class="hpkc\_\_divider"><span class="hpkc\_\_diamond"></span></div>

&#x20;       </div>



&#x20;       <div class="hpkc\_\_split">

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_panel">

&#x20;           <span class="hpkc\_\_panel-ic">🔊</span>

&#x20;           <h3>A Deeper, Richer Sound</h3>

&#x20;           <p>Thick PBT walls dampen the high-pitched ping of thinner caps,

&#x20;              giving every keystroke a fuller, more grounded "thock" that feels

&#x20;              as premium as it sounds.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_panel">

&#x20;           <span class="hpkc\_\_panel-ic">🤚</span>

&#x20;           <h3>A Textured, Grippy Touch</h3>

&#x20;           <p>The fine matte texture adds confident grip and a tactile warmth,

&#x20;              while resisting the greasy shine that smooth ABS caps develop

&#x20;              over time.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </section>

&#x20;     <!-- ===================== INSTALLATION ===================== -->

&#x20;     <section class="hpkc\_\_section">

&#x20;       <div class="hpkc\_\_head">

&#x20;         <div class="hpkc\_\_kicker">Easy Setup</div>

&#x20;         <h2 class="hpkc\_\_h2">Casting the Spell — Installation</h2>

&#x20;         <p class="hpkc\_\_lead">No wand required. Swap your keycaps in minutes

&#x20;           with the included puller.</p>

&#x20;         <div class="hpkc\_\_divider"><span class="hpkc\_\_diamond"></span></div>

&#x20;       </div>



&#x20;       <div class="hpkc\_\_steps">

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_step">

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_step-num">01</div>

&#x20;           <h4>Pull the Old Caps</h4>

&#x20;           <p>Slide the wire keycap puller over each cap and lift straight up to

&#x20;              remove your existing keycaps.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_step">

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_step-num">02</div>

&#x20;           <h4>Match the Layout</h4>

&#x20;           <p>Lay out the new set to match your board, lining up modifiers and

&#x20;              the spacebar before mounting.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_step">

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_step-num">03</div>

&#x20;           <h4>Press Into Place</h4>

&#x20;           <p>Align each cap over its switch stem and press down gently until it

&#x20;              clicks securely into position.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_step">

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_step-num">04</div>

&#x20;           <h4>Cast Away</h4>

&#x20;           <p>Give your board a quick once-over, then enjoy a desk that looks

&#x20;              and feels truly magical.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </section>



&#x20;     <!-- ===================== CARE GUIDE ===================== -->

&#x20;     <section class="hpkc\_\_section">

&#x20;       <div class="hpkc\_\_head">

&#x20;         <div class="hpkc\_\_kicker">Keep the Magic Alive</div>

&#x20;         <h2 class="hpkc\_\_h2">Care \&amp; Keeping</h2>

&#x20;         <div class="hpkc\_\_divider"><span class="hpkc\_\_diamond"></span></div>

&#x20;       </div>



&#x20;       <div class="hpkc\_\_care">

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_care-card">

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_care-num">01</div>

&#x20;           <h4>Gentle Cleaning</h4>

&#x20;           <p>Wipe keycaps with a soft, slightly damp cloth. For a deeper

&#x20;              clean, remove them and rinse in warm soapy water.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_care-card">

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_care-num">02</div>

&#x20;           <h4>Dry Fully</h4>

&#x20;           <p>Always let keycaps dry completely before remounting them on your

&#x20;              switches to protect your board.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_care-card">

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_care-num">03</div>

&#x20;           <h4>Use a Puller</h4>

&#x20;           <p>Remove caps with a wire keycap puller to avoid scratching the

&#x20;              legends or bending the stems.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;         <article class="hpkc\_\_care-card">

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_care-num">04</div>

&#x20;           <h4>Avoid Harsh Chemicals</h4>

&#x20;           <p>Skip solvents and abrasive cleaners. PBT and dye-sub legends only

&#x20;              need water and a little care.</p>

&#x20;         </article>

&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </section>

&#x20;     <!-- ===================== FAQ ===================== -->

&#x20;     <section class="hpkc\_\_section">

&#x20;       <div class="hpkc\_\_head">

&#x20;         <div class="hpkc\_\_kicker">Answers</div>

&#x20;         <h2 class="hpkc\_\_h2">Frequently Asked Questions</h2>

&#x20;         <div class="hpkc\_\_divider"><span class="hpkc\_\_diamond"></span></div>

&#x20;       </div>



&#x20;       <div class="hpkc\_\_faq">



&#x20;         <details open>

&#x20;           <summary>Will this fit my keyboard?</summary>

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;             The set supports most standard layouts including 61, 64, 68, 75,

&#x20;             84, 87, 96, 98, 100, 104 and 108-key boards. It is not compatible

&#x20;             with 6U or 6.5U spacebars or ultra-thin layouts. If you're unsure,

&#x20;             send us your keyboard model and we'll confirm before you order.

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </details>



&#x20;         <details>

&#x20;           <summary>What switches does it work with?</summary>

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;             The keycaps use a standard MX-style stem, so they're compatible

&#x20;             with Cherry MX, Gateron, Kailh and most other MX-style switches.

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </details>



&#x20;         <details>

&#x20;           <summary>What's the difference between Cherry, MOA and SOA?</summary>

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;             They're different keycap profiles — essentially the height and

&#x20;             shape of the keys. Cherry is low and classic, MOA is a comfortable

&#x20;             medium-height sculpt, and SOA is a taller, spherical profile with a

&#x20;             vintage feel. Choose whichever typing experience you prefer.

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </details>



&#x20;         <details>

&#x20;           <summary>Will the legends fade over time?</summary>

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;             No. The legends are dye-sublimated, meaning the design is infused

&#x20;             into the PBT surface rather than printed on top. They won't peel,

&#x20;             smudge or fade with everyday use.

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </details>



&#x20;         <details>

&#x20;           <summary>How do I install the keycaps?</summary>

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;             It's simple. Use a keycap puller to remove your old caps, line up

&#x20;             the new set with your layout, and press each cap onto its switch

&#x20;             stem until it clicks. The whole swap usually takes just a few

&#x20;             minutes.

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </details>



&#x20;         <details>

&#x20;           <summary>How should I clean them?</summary>

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;             Wipe with a soft, slightly damp cloth for everyday cleaning. For a

&#x20;             deeper clean, remove the caps and rinse them in warm soapy water,

&#x20;             then dry fully before remounting. Avoid harsh solvents.

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </details>



&#x20;         <details>

&#x20;           <summary>Does this include a keyboard?</summary>

&#x20;           <div class="hpkc\_\_faq-body">

&#x20;             This listing is for the keycap set only. It's designed to dress up

&#x20;             a mechanical keyboard you already own.

&#x20;           </div>

&#x20;         </details>



&#x20;       </div>

&#x20;     </section>

<script>

/\* The closing seal scrolls the page to the store's Add to Cart button.

&#x20;  Works across most Shopify themes; falls back to the #AddToCart anchor

&#x20;  if a theme blocks inline scripts. \*/

(function () {

&#x20; function bind() {

&#x20;   var link = document.getElementById('hpkcCartLink');

&#x20;   if (!link) return;

&#x20;   link.addEventListener('click', function (e) {

&#x20;     var selectors = \[

&#x20;       'button\[name="add"]',

&#x20;       '\[name="add"]',

&#x20;       '.product-form\_\_submit',

&#x20;       '.product-form\_\_cart-submit',

&#x20;       '#AddToCart',

&#x20;       '#AddToCart-product-template',

&#x20;       '.add-to-cart',

&#x20;       '\[data-add-to-cart]',

&#x20;       'form\[action\*="/cart/add"] \[type="submit"]',

&#x20;       'form\[action\*="/cart/add"] button'

&#x20;     ];

&#x20;     var target = null;

&#x20;     for (var i = 0; i < selectors.length \&\& !target; i++) {

&#x20;       target = document.querySelector(selectors\[i]);

&#x20;     }

&#x20;     if (target) {

&#x20;       e.preventDefault();

&#x20;       target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });

&#x20;     }

&#x20;   });

&#x20; }

&#x20; if (document.readyState !== 'loading') { bind(); }

&#x20; else { document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', bind); }

})();

</script>
